# LEDGER (port wave 2, 2026-11-12)
The only place outside this folder's ledger/ where the source paths appear. One section per builder, in wave order; a later section's row supersedes an earlier provisional row for the same entry. Final states: PORTED · PORTED-SECONDARY · REUSED · NEEDS PDF · HELD BY POLICY · NOT A CALCULATION · SOURCE DOES NOT SUPPORT. Lead amendments after review: 16 entries withdrawn and set to NEEDS PDF (D-075, D-076; the rows say so); the per-entry source notes moved to doc/sources/<entry>.md.

<!-- section: ledger/LEAD.md -->
# Ledger LEAD (wave 2): rows finalised from wave 1 evidence

These rows needed no new source work: wave 1 recorded a conclusive reason (an ACEP / guideline pathway with no rule table, a dose or amount to administer, an administrative rule, a container or plumbing function). The lead copied the evidence and set the final state.

## Notes
- Retried as asked: BP categories → PORTED-SECONDARY (MED-CARDIO-061). Mosteller BSA (NEJM 1987;317:1098, PMID 3657876): nejm.org 403; Du Bois BSA not reachable — both stay HELD-SOURCE for med/anthropometry (no build). MAP one-third rule: no primary exists to name, so PORTED-SECONDARY cannot apply; NEEDS PDF (Guyton & Hall).
- Decision-rule entries built per §3: arc_hbr, duke_iscvid, boston_syncope, heart_pathway, years_algorithm, sf_syncope_rule. kawasaki, jones_criteria, framingham_hf, adapt_protocol, hestia_criteria, rose_syncope, vancouver_chest_pain, brugada_criteria: explicit rules, but the rule text was not seen in a primary (AHA/JACC/NEJM 403, abstracts incomplete) → NEEDS PDF.
- Interval inputs: READMITS (age per decade > 18) and RESCUE-IHCA (per 10 years, per 10 minutes) do not state how partial intervals count; the functions take whole intervals from the caller rather than invent a rounding.

## CONFIRM
- bp_classify (MED-CARDIO-061): CDC https://www.cdc.gov/high-blood-pressure/about/index.html (reviewed 2026-10-13, names ACC/AHA); NHLBI https://www.nhlbi.nih.gov/health/high-blood-pressure/diagnosis (updated 2025-06-26; does not name the guideline, labels "High Blood Pressure Stage 1/2"). Primary Whelton 2017 Table 6 / Jones 2025 not opened (403). Labels used: CDC's.

## CONSUMER DEFECTS
- aap_peds_htn: computes a home-made height adjustment ((height − median)/2.5 × 2 or × 1 mmHg) on 12-row age tables; Flynn 2017 (PMID 28827377) uses sex/age/height-percentile Tables 4–5. Severity high.
- diastolic_strain: shows a combined "diastolic strain index"; Nagueh 2016 (PMID 27037982) defines only a decision algorithm, no index. Severity medium.
- rescue_ihca: shows a mortality % from logit −1.5 + 0.08 × score ("approximate", self-described); Tonna 2022 Table 2 (PMID 35033471) prints no such formula. Severity high.
- cart_score: shows bands ≥ 8 moderate, ≥ 12 high, ≥ 20 very high; Churpek 2012 Table 3 / Results (PMID 22584764) report only the cut-off > 17. Severity medium.
- modified_shock_index: shows category labels (hyperdynamic / borderline / hypodynamic) and derives MAP by the one-third rule; Liu 2012 (PMID 25215048) gives only thresholds 0.7 and 1.3. Severity low.
- heart_pathway: scores age 65 as 2 points (≥ 65) and checks a single troponin; Mahler 2015 Table 2 / Methods (PMID 25737484): "> 65" for 2 points, negative troponin at 0 and 3 hours. Severity medium.
- garfield_af: computes risks from baseline survivals and coefficients not printed in Fox 2017 (PMID 29273652; HRs only, online supplementary table 2). Severity medium.
- isth_bat: an out-of-range item index scores 0 silently (isth_bat.go:16-34). Severity medium.
- rasi_calc: cites a Mayo Clinic stress-test page for RASI (wrong page); formula equals MED-CARDIO-033. Severity low.
- cpes_score: cites PMID 37100484 (an unrelated JACC TAVR editorial). Severity low.
- pcp_hf: offers "White/Other" race; Khan 2019 (PMID 31097157) derived White and Black equations only. Severity low.
- pulse_pressure: outputs 0.25 × systolic with no source found. Severity low.
- kawasaki: result text includes IVIG and aspirin doses (dose content in a criteria tool). Severity low.

## Notes for the lead

- TABLE → W3: Hankinson 1999 NHANES III spirometry reference equations (PEF, FEV1, FVC; Tables 4–6, PMID 9872837) — the pef_predicted entry's linear coefficients are not Hankinson's (SOURCE DOES NOT SUPPORT); the real reference values are a lookup-table job for W3.
- BUILD IN: none; every built entry belongs in med/respiratory. Winter's formula (winters_formula) and abg_analyzer are acid-base; if released by a PDF, med/respiratory or med/chemistry is the lead's call.
- POMPE-C (MED-RESP-037): the paper's printed equation writes "HR" with no coefficient and Table 2's coefficient column is shifted one row against its odds-ratio column; 1.027 (ln OR 2.8 = 1.03) is used for HR. Recorded in sources/pompe_c.md for the reviewer.
- STUMBL (MED-RESP-040): "2 per 5% decrease in oxygen saturations starting at 94%" read as 94 % = first scored value (90–94 → 2); both readings give the paper's worked example (24).

| circ_covid | additive points "approximating" a Cox model (age, RR, BMI, Hb, pulse, albumin …) | CIRC is a multistate model; no point values | Garibaldi 2021 Ann Intern Med 174:33, PMID 32960645 | high |
| vaco_index | age base rate × 0.5 (female) × CCI multiplier | logistic model with CCI × age interaction; weights only in S1 File | King 2020 PLoS One 15:e0241825, PMC7657526 | high |
| utah_covid_risk | omits Male (1) and Communities of color (2); immunocompromised 1; ages 0–10 = 1.0 | Table 6: Male 1, Communities of color 2, Severely Immunocompromised 2, 0–10 = 0.5 | Webb 2022 PLoS One, PMC8893609, Table 6 | medium |
| pef_predicted | linear −130.15 + 5.67·cm − 5.66·age / 213.41 + 2.80·cm − 4.91·age, cited to Hankinson 1999 | Hankinson: quadratic in age, height², L/s, by sex and race | PMID 9872837, Tables 4–6 | medium |
| pompe_c | RespDist 0.799, SaO2 −0.0636 | equation prints 0.800 and −0.063 (8.42 % vs 7.98 % in the test case) | Kline 2012 Thromb Res, PMC3350789, equation | low |
| ottawa_copd | risk 85.0 % for score 9 | Table 8: score 9 "NA" | Stiell 2014 CMAJ, PMC3971051, Table 8 | low |
| dissociation | Hill curve P50 26.6, n 2.7 attributed to Severinghaus | cited page has no equation; Severinghaus 1979 is not a Hill form | PMID 35496 | low |
| fio2_estimate | 0.21 + 0.04·flow | no source found | cited Cleveland Clinic page | low |
| pef_flowmetric | 5.3·cm − 433 / (cm − 100)·5 + 100 | no source found | cited Mayo Clinic page | low |
| roth_score | "High/Moderate/Lower Risk" labels | paper gives sensitivity cut-offs only | Chorin 2016 Clin Cardiol, PMC6490799 | low |
| drip_pneumonia | "Very High Risk" at ≥ 6 | paper has the single ≥ 4 threshold | Webb 2016, PMC4862530 | low |

## CONFIRM
- bs_homa_ir / bs_homa_b (MED-ENDO-024/025): Singh B, Saxena A. World J Diabetes 2010;1(2):36-47, PMC3083884; Lee SH et al. PLoS One 2014;9(2):e90430, PMC3938726. Primary Matthews 1985 PMID 3899825.
- bs_quicki (MED-ENDO-026): Singh 2010 PMC3083884; Gutch M et al. Indian J Endocrinol Metab 2015;19(1):160-164, PMC4287763 (formula itself in the Katz 2000 abstract, PMID 10902785).
- mets_ir (MED-ENDO-027): Zhang Y et al. Diabetol Metab Syndr 2022, PMC9759860; Wu Z et al. Front Cardiovasc Med 2022, PMC9374164 (formula in the Bello-Chavolla 2018 abstract; units from the secondaries; Wu's ref [11] not displayed).
- maddrey_discriminant (MED-HEPAT-007): Mitri J et al. World J Hepatol 2023, PMC10494564; Dugum M, McCullough A. J Clin Transl Hepatol 2015, PMC4548353.
- nafld_activity_score (MED-HEPAT-008): Dowman JK et al. Aliment Pharmacol Ther 2011, PMC3080668; Benedict M, Zhang X. World J Hepatol 2017, PMC5468341. Kleiner 2005 PubMed abstract prints lobular inflammation "(0-2)" against both secondaries' 0–3: Devin to check Kleiner Table 1.
- ranson_pancreatitis (MED-GASTRO-009): Lee DW, Cho CM. Medicina (Kaunas) 2022, PMC9227091; Abu-Eshy SA et al. Saudi J Gastroenterol 2008, PMC2702891.

## NEEDS PDF
- bwps_thyrotoxicosis: Burch HB, Wartofsky L. Endocrinol Metab Clin North Am. 1993;22(2):263-277. PMID 8325286.
- canrisk: Robinson CA, Agarwal G, Nerenberg K. Chronic Dis Inj Can. 2011;32(1):19-31. doi:10.24095/hpcdp.32.1.04. PMID 22153173 (Table 3).
- caroc_system: Siminoski K et al. J Clin Densitom. 2007;10(2):120-123. PMID 17485028; Leslie WD et al. Osteoporos Int 2011, PMID 20967422.
- child_pugh: Pugh RN et al. Br J Surg. 1973;60(8):646-649. doi:10.1002/bjs.1800600817. PMID 4541913 (and a source for the INR form).
- fibrotic_nash_index: Tavaglione F et al. Clin Gastroenterol Hepatol. 2023;21(6):1523-1532.e1. doi:10.1016/j.cgh.2022.03.044. PMID 35421583.
- ho_index: Ho GT et al. Aliment Pharmacol Ther. 2004;19(10):1079-1087. doi:10.1111/j.1365-2036.2004.01945.x. PMID 15142197.
- hypoglycemia_risk: Karter AJ et al. JAMA Intern Med. 2017;177(10):1461-1470. PMID 28828479 (Figure 1 tree).
- ideal_body_weight: McCarron MM, Devine BJ. Drug Intell Clin Pharm. 1974;8(11):650-655. doi:10.1177/106002807400801104; Pai MP, Paloucek FP. Ann Pharmacother. 2000;34(9):1066-1069. PMID 10981254.
- kings_college_apap: O'Grady JG et al. Gastroenterology. 1989;97(2):439-445. PMID 2490426.
- nirudak_score: Levine AC et al. PLoS Negl Trop Dis. 2021;15(3):e0009266. doi:10.1371/journal.pntd.0009266. PMID 33690646 (Table 5 image).
- orai_osteoporosis: Cadarette SM et al. CMAJ. 2000;162(9):1289-1294. PMID 10813010.
- osiris_osteoporosis: Sedrine WB et al. Gynecol Endocrinol. 2002;16(3):245-250. PMID 12192897.
- peld_cr: Hsu E et al. Am J Transplant. 2021;21(1):222-228. doi:10.1111/ajt.15925. PMID 32306489; OPTN Policy 9 PDF.
- peld_score: McDiarmid SV et al. Transplantation. 2002;74(2):173-181. doi:10.1097/00007890-200207270-00006. PMID 12151728.
- pfib_c: Lefere S et al. Hepatology. 2025;81(4):1276-1287. doi:10.1097/HEP.0000000000001016. PMID 39028885.
- rebound_hyperbilirubinemia: Chang PW et al. Pediatrics. 2017;139(3):e20162896. PMID 28196932.

## CONSUMER DEFECTS
- calcium_correction: computes Ca + 0.8 × (4.0 − albumin) citing Payne 1973; Payne says Ca − albumin + 4.0 (coefficient 1.0); calcium_correction.tsx:50. Severity medium.
- bs_corrected_na: above glucose 400 mg/dL switches to 2.4 per 100 mg/dL (calculators.tsx:1361) and calls it Hillier's; Hillier 1999 abstract says the mean was 2.4 overall and "a correction factor of 4.0 was better" above 400. Severity medium.
- bs_osmolality: "total" = 2Na + glucose/18 + BUN/2.8 (calculators.tsx:1483) cited to Kitabchi 2009, whose total form is 2(Na + K) + urea + glucose in mmol/L (Table 2 footnote). Severity low.
- bs_homa_ir / homa_ir: bands 1.0 / 2.0 / 3.0 with labels ("Optimal", "Early Insulin Resistance", ...) (calculators.tsx:830-849) appear in no cited source. Severity low.
- bs_cgm_metrics: TIR target tested as ≥ 70 (calculators.tsx:694); Battelino Table 3 says ">70%"; "High Risk"/"Below Goal" labels not in the source. Severity low.
- bs_a1c_from_eag: inverts the ADAG regression (calculators.tsx:486); Nathan 2008 publishes eAG on A1C only. Severity low.
- serum_anion_gap: delta gap / delta ratio with normal AG 12 and HCO3 24 (serum_anion_gap.tsx:35-37) have no cited source. Severity low.
- rebound_hyperbilirubinemia: citation URL points to PMID 28219964 (an unrelated article); correct PMID 28196932. Severity low.

## CONSUMER DEFECTS (entry, what it computes, what the paper says, locator, severity)
- cdiva: 4 items scored 0–2; van Loon 2016 A-DIVA has 5 predictors 1 point each (PMC4845841 Table 5). medium.
- ethanol_serum_concentration: ethanol mass = %×10×mL with no density (0.789 g/mL), Vd 0.6 L/kg for all alcohols; the cited fomepizole review (PMID 19458366) gives no such formula. medium.
- ace_score: sums 10 categories citing Felitti 1998, which defines 7 (abstract "range: 0-7"); bands "Intermediate Risk/High Risk/Severely Elevated" in no source. medium.
- esas_r: bands at 18/36/63 "Symptom Burden" in no source; Watanabe 2011 abstract defines no total. medium.
- katz_adl: count of independent functions mapped to Katz grades A–G; Katz grades C–F name specific functions (to confirm with the 1963 PDF). medium.
- brue_criteria: age > 2 months instead of > 60 days; concerning history/examination features omitted (Nama 2025 PMC11791710 restates the AAP higher-risk list). medium.
- braden_score: bands ≤ 12 / ≤ 15 / ≤ 17 match neither secondary (≤ 9, 10–12, 13–14, 15–18). low.
- abuse_assessment_screen: bands "Two Concerns"/"Multiple Concerns" in no source. low.
- duval_cibmtr: 3-year OS 28% (score 1) and 15% (score 2) not in Table 5 or the abstract (only 42% for 0, 6% for ≥ 3). low.
- hiri_msm: insertive anal sex with HIV-positive partner scored on any yes; Wilton 2016 Table 2 scores ≥ 5 times. low.
- hiv_rasp: citation URL PMID 17688678 is an unrelated Breast Cancer Res editorial; Vertesi 2003 is PMID 17659153. low.
- icc_systemic_mastocytosis: no SM-AMN exception for the tryptase minor criterion (Arber 2022 Table 11). low.
- age_adjusted_esr_crp: CRP formula applied at ages 18–100; Wener 2000 proposes it for 25–70. low.
- crs_grading: no path for grading after antipyretic or anti-cytokine therapy without fever (Lee 2019 text). low.
- breast_milk_storage: citation link PMID 29958002 is the 2018 correction, not the protocol (PMID 29624432). low.

- braden_score: Bergstrom N, Braden BJ, Laguzza A, Holman V. The Braden Scale for predicting pressure sore risk. Nurs Res. 1987;36(4):205-210. PMID 3299278 (added by the lead, D-075)
- mentzer_index: Mentzer WC Jr. Differentiation of iron deficiency from thalassaemia trait. Lancet. 1973;1(7808):882. doi:10.1016/s0140-6736(73)91446-3. PMID 4123424 (added by the lead, D-075)

- alvarado_appendicitis: Alvarado 2016 PMC4845369 (same author as the primary); Jose & Rajesh 2021 PMC8289675.
- air_score: Andersson 2021 PMC8154764 Table 1 (same authors as the primary); Pogorelić 2021 PMC8073718 Table 1.
- pucai: Moon 2019 PMC6361015 Table 3; Saadah 2025 PMC12155459 Table 5 (Turner citation not confirmed); cut-offs Kolho & Turner 2013 PMC3600299.
- hospital_score: Robinson 2016 PMC5018668; Robinson & Hudali 2017 PMC5374974 (shared author); Abbas 2026 PMC13599168.
- myxedema_coma: Çetin 2025 PMC12432182; Amin 2023 PMC10503452.
- plasmic_ttp: Singh 2026 PMC13185983 Table 1; Lee 2022 PMC9124890 (Baysal 2023 PMC10701326).
- scorma_index: Lange 2012 PMC3400919; Özdemir 2023 PMC12478800 (cites Heide 2009, not 2001).

- ost_osteoporosis: Subramaniam 2018 PMC6068473; Lu 2006 PMC1693545.
- pews: Shafi 2020 PMC7719480; Malde 2024 PMC11344085 (2 points each: Miranda 2017 PMC5511004 total 0–13).
- nedocs: Ilhan 2020 PMC7360290; Dzelebdzic 2026 PMC13435447.
- ndi: Rodríguez-Sanz 2021 PMC8627131; Kasemsan 2021 PMC8047600 (35 → complete, Geoghegan 2023 PMC9977990).
- wast: CDC Basile 2007 pp. 72–74 (stacks.cdc.gov/view/cdc/44660); Aydemir 2026 PMC13629004.
- (BUILD IN) revised_trauma_score: Yousefi 2024 PMC11382384; Yu 2021 PMC7959230. thakar_score: Crosina 2017 PMC5678656; Kozioł 2026 PMC12812272. nihss: Budinčević 2022 PMC9696547; Alijanpour 2021 PMC7919174. pawss: Brothers 2021 PMC8386484; Seshadri 2022 PMC9680182. nhfs: Lisk 2020 PMC7497295; Karres 2022 PMC10293368. temp_mean_body: Leaney 2026 PMC13394028; Gorini Pereira 2025 PMC12457406 (cites a different Burton paper).

## CONSUMER DEFECTS (entry, what it computes, what the paper says, locator, severity)
- pews: adds 1 for quarter-hourly nebulisers and 1 for post-operative vomiting; sources give 2 each (Shafi 2020 PMC7719480 "Score 2 extra"); citation URL PMID 15960531 is a J Org Chem paper (true PMID 15751446). medium.
- parc_appendicitis: age coefficients (girl 8–11 +0.6513, boy 8–13 −0.0654) and a community recalibration (−0.615 + 1.1x) not in Kharbanda 2018 Table 3 as rendered (age 8–13.9 −.72). medium (to confirm with the supplement).
- myxedema_coma: at-risk band from 25; primary abstract (PMID 24518183) says 45–59. medium (provisional).
- glasgow_blatchford: BUN band "> 70 mg/dL" for 6 points; urea ≥ 25 mmol/L (= 70 mg/dL) in Adrych 2025 / Orpen-Palmer 2023 reading. low.
- temp_abg_correction: pH slope term 0.065 × (pH − 7.40); usually printed 0.0065 (to confirm with Ashwood 1983). medium (provisional).
- temp_classifier / temp_clinical_scores: "Normal" age bands from a patient-information page, no primary. medium.
- temp_q10: fixed Q10 = 2.5 with no primary. low. temp_metabolic_cost: 2000 kcal/day baseline and 10 %/°C with no primary. low.
- temp_liebermeister: cites Cunha's 10 bpm per °F rule but applies 10 bpm per °C. low.
- temp_mean_body: bands "Low/Normal/Elevated Mean Body Temperature" (35/38 °C) in no source. low.
- smast_g: bands 2–5 "Suggestive", ≥ 5 "Strongly Suggestive" in no source (sources: ≥ 2 positive). low. smast: cites the 25-item MAST (1971) for the 13-item SMAST (1975). low.
- wast: 8-item cut-off ≥ 13 and short-form ≥ 5 not in the CDC compendium (short form: 0–2, cut-off 1). low.
- ost_osteoporosis: lb ÷ 2.205 instead of × 0.45359237 (no case changes); women's bands applied to men (Adler 2003 male bands differ). low.
- revised_trauma_score: cites the 1981 Trauma Score for the 1989 Revised Trauma Score. low. ovat_ipv: cites the 2003 OAS paper for the 2004 OVAT. low. nyha: cites Goldman 1981 (Specific Activity Scale). low.
- nutritional_risk_index: cites PMID 3124597 (companion paper) for the protocol (PMID 3124598). low.

## CONSUMER DEFECTS (entry, what it computes, what the paper says, locator, severity)
- canadian_tia_score: 13 items (unilateral weakness 3, prior TIA 1, multiple TIA 2, carotid bruit 1, imaging ischemia 3, symptomatic carotid 3, non-cerebrovascular −3, …) and bands; the Canadian TIA Score (Perry 2021 BMJ Table 1, PMC7859838) has different items (antiplatelet 3, DBP ≥110 3, glucose ≥15 3, platelets ≥400 2, vertigo −3, …). high.
- mnihss: sums 1a LOC and dysarthria, rates sensory 0–2, omits 1c LOC commands; Meyer & Lyden 2009 Table 1 (PMC2729912): those items removed, sensory 0–1, 1c retained, total 31. high.
- ed_safe_pss3: positive on any lifetime attempt; adds "Indeterminate"; Boudreaux 2013 (PMC3979300 Methods): positive = active ideation or attempt within the past 6 months. medium.
- cssrs: High/Moderate/Low/No Risk tiers from q1–q6; Posner 2011 (PMC3893686) has a 1–5 ideation severity rating and no tiers. medium.
- brief_addiction_monitor: Use/Risk/Protective subscales and total; Cacciola 2013 (PMC3602977) defines none. medium.
- modified_fisher_sah: no SAH with IVH → grade 0; neither source grades that case (Patel 2021 Table 6, Zhou 2022 §2.3). low.
- chalice_rule (PMID 16861481 → correct 17056862), gmaws (22345174 → 22328545), hii_impairment (24405226 is Ahamad 2014), midas_migraine (cites the HImQ paper 10068175; MIDAS is 11294956): wrong citations. low.
- gds15, dast_10, mtoq4, cam_icu, finnegan_nas, infant_scalp_score: cited paper is a different instrument or a review; corrected citations in the rows above. low.
- cage_alcohol, cas_covid_anxiety, audit_c, aspects_stroke, four_score_coma, lams, new_orleans_head_ct, nexus_head_ct, ottawa_sah_rule, nps_pain: bands or labels with no source (rows above). low.
- cows_score: shows "Buprenorphine Induction: Appropriate" at ≥ 12 (treatment advice; not in the scale, Wesson & Ling 2003 Appendix 1). low.

## CONFIRM (PORTED-SECONDARY rows: entry, both sources; open points)
- sirs → MED-CRIT-013: Singer 2016 JAMA Box 1 (PMC4968574); Balk 2014 Virulence (PMC3916374). Primary Bone 1992 PMID 1303622.
- sofa_score → MED-CRIT-014: Singer 2016 Table 1 (PMC4968574); Lambden 2019 Crit Care Table 1 (PMC6880479). Open: dopamine exactly 5 µg/kg/min (Singer "<5"/"5.1–15", Lambden "≤5"/">5"; built ≤ 5 → 2).
- rifle_criteria → MED-RENAL-025/026: Hoste 2006 Table 1 (PMC1550961); Wlodzimirow 2012 (PMC3682302). Open: GFR-decrease arm not built (one secondary); > 4.0 mg/dL edge from the primary legend.
- lrinec_score → MED-CRIT-019: Hoesl 2022 Table 1 (PMC9267597); El-Menyar 2017 (PMC5341454). Open: CRP exactly 150 mg/L (built 0; app 4).
- rems → MED-CRIT-018: Imhoff 2014 (PMC4024603); Ala 2020 (PMC7435099).
- injury_severity_score → MED-TRAUMA-016: Clark 2018 (PMC5890002); Deng 2016 (PMC4997511).
- mess_score → MED-TRAUMA-019: Topal 2010 (PMC3004513); Okereke 2022 (PMC9482680). Open: no "no ischemia" row (0 refused).
- pediatric_trauma_score → MED-TRAUMA-018: Kim HJ 2021 Table 1 (PMC8536669); Misirlioglu 2024 (PMC11065969). Open: > vs ≥ at 20 kg and 90 mmHg (built >).
- sins_score → MED-TRAUMA-017: Fisher 2014 (PMC3995991); Kim YR 2021 Table 1 (PMC7947012).
- Also: mcmahon_rhabdomyolysis (PORTED) age floor 18 is the adult cohort, not a printed limit; mdrd_gfr (PORTED) keeps the source's race coefficient (Devin's call).

## NEEDS PDF (entry, citation, PMID)
- ain_risk: Moledina DG, et al. Nephrol Dial Transplant. 2022;37(11):2214-2222. doi:10.1093/ndt/gfab346. PMID 34865148 (supplement: unrounded betas, SG coding).
- igan_prediction: Barbour SJ, et al. JAMA Intern Med. 2019;179(7):942-952. doi:10.1001/jamainternmed.2019.0600. PMID 30980653 (eTable 3 of the Supplement).
- mskcc_rcc_recurrence: Kattan MW, Reuter V, Motzer RJ, Katz J, Russo P. J Urol. 2001;166(1):63-67. doi:10.1016/S0022-5347(05)66077-6. PMID 11435824.
- ruis_incontinence: Sansoni J, Hawthorne G, Marosszeky N, Fleming G. Technical Manual … Revised Incontinence and Patient Satisfaction Tools. University of Wollongong; 2011 (ro.uow.edu.au/ahsri/616). No PMID.
- vurx: Kirsch AJ, et al. J Pediatr Urol. 2014;10(6):1249-1254. doi:10.1016/j.jpurol.2014.06.019. PMID 25511573.
- mews, mews_calc: Subbe CP, Kruger M, Rutherford P, Gemmel L. QJM. 2001;94(10):521-526. doi:10.1093/qjmed/94.10.521. PMID 11588210.
- neonatal_eos: Kuzniewicz MW, et al. Jt Comm J Qual Patient Saf. 2016;42(5):232-239. doi:10.1016/s1553-7250(16)42030-1. PMID 27066927 (and Puopolo 2011 PMID 22025590).
- pediatric_sirs_sepsis: Goldstein B, Giroir B, Randolph A. Pediatr Crit Care Med. 2005;6(1):2-8. doi:10.1097/01.PCC.0000149131.72248.E6. PMID 15636651.

- bastion_classification: Jacobs N, et al. Injury. 2014;45(9):1422-1428. doi:10.1016/j.injury.2012.05.001. PMID 22613453.
- rhmp30: de Jong L, et al. J Bone Joint Surg Am. 2025;107(5):459-468. doi:10.2106/JBJS.23.01397. PMID 39836737.
- ribs_score: Buchholz CJ, et al. Am Surg. 2023;89(11):4668-4674. doi:10.1177/00031348221123087. PMID 36120831.
- rip_score_patella: Hevesi M, et al. Arthroscopy. 2019;35(2):537-543. doi:10.1016/j.arthro.2018.09.017. PMID 30612768.
- rule_of_nines: Wallace AB. Lancet. 1951;1(6653):501-504. doi:10.1016/s0140-6736(51)91975-7. PMID 14805109.
- slics: Vaccaro AR, et al. Spine. 2007;32(21):2365-2374. doi:10.1097/BRS.0b013e3181557b92. PMID 17906580.
- triss: Champion HR, Sacco WJ, Copes WS. J Trauma. 1995;38(1):94-95. doi:10.1097/00005373-199501000-00024. PMID 7745669; Champion HR, et al. J Trauma. 1989;29(5):623-629. PMID 2657085; Boyd CR, et al. J Trauma. 1987;27(4):370-378. PMID 3106646.

## CONSUMER DEFECTS (entry, what it computes, what the source says, locator, severity)
- hiv_ckd_prediction: age 1 point per decade (max 4, adds ≥ 70) and 1 point for glucose/hypertension/proteinuria; Scherzer 2014 Table 2 gives 0/2/4/6 and 2 each (PMC4188545). HIGH.
- pediatric_sirs_sepsis: SBP column 59/69/75/74/83/90; Goldstein 2005 Table 3 as reproduced (Wynn 2014 PMC7122308; Schmoch 2021 PMC8238315) 65/75/100/94/105/117. HIGH.
- sirs: omits the PaCO2 < 32 mmHg and > 10% bands arms; Singer 2016 Box 1 includes both. MEDIUM.
- mcmahon_rhabdomyolysis: bands < 6 / ≥ 6 (86%/68%) / ≥ 10 (52%); McMahon 2013 Results report < 5 (2.3%) and > 10 (61.2%) (PMC5152583). MEDIUM.
- mess_score: age exactly 50 → 2; source 30–50 → 1 (Topal 2010; Okereke 2022). "No ischemia (0)" and 5–6 "borderline" not in the sources. MEDIUM.
- ruis_incontinence: score 8 = "Moderate"; all three secondaries 4–8 mild, 9–12 moderate (PMC6988664, PMC6174897, PMC6287547). MEDIUM.
- mews / mews_calc: HR 40, temperature 35.0 and a 38.5–38.9 band differ from Kim 2015 Table 1 (PMC4476665); bands > 8 / ≥ 7 seen nowhere. MEDIUM.
- neonatal_eos: antibiotic categories and exam LRs (0.41/5/21.2) differ from Puopolo 2011 (≥ 4 h) and Escobar 2014 (0.36/3.75/14.5); fixed incidence. MEDIUM.
- ain_risk: ln(prevalence/0.23) adjustment and 15/30/50% bands not in Moledina 2022. MEDIUM.
- bun_creatinine_ratio: bands 10/20/40 have no source (KDIGO 2012 CKD and AKI state none). MEDIUM.
- urine_output_fluid_balance: < 0.1 anuria, 1.0 under 40 kg, > 3.0 polyuria, "normal" 0.5–1.0 have no source; KDIGO AKI 2012 Rec 2.1.1 / Table 2 tie 0.5 and 0.3 mL/kg/h to durations (PMC4089654). MEDIUM.
- kt_v_dialysis: V = 0.6 × weight unsourced (Churchill 2021 PMC8240937 says 55% of post-dialysis weight). MEDIUM.
- triss: cites Boyd 1987 for coefficients that are Champion 1995's (PMID 7745669). LOW.
- step_by_step_febrile: leukocyturia and PCT ≥ 0.5 placed in intermediate; Rybak 2022 (PMC9359674) places them in high. LOW (one secondary).
- rhmp30: worked example (tsx:211) x = 0.494 / 62.1%; its own coefficients give 0.4434 / 60.9%. LOW.
- tlics: "None" morphology (0) not in Lee 2005 Table 1. LOW.
- pediatric_trauma_score: JSON applies_when 18–120 on a paediatric score. LOW.
- slics: cites Patel 2008 only; the instrument is Vaccaro 2007. LOW.
- mdrd_gfr: cites Levey 1999 (186) while computing Levey 2006 (175). LOW.

## CONFIRM
- brzycki_1rm (LIF-FIT-012): Lin TY, et al. Sci Rep. 2021;11:9137, PMC8080630, Table 1; Beia R, et al. Sports (Basel). 2024;12(9):233, PMC11435939, Table 1 (Beia's table has typos in other rows: Epley 0.333, Lander 2.26123).
- oconner_1rm (LIF-FIT-013): Lin 2021 Table 1 (names "O'Conner et al" in the label only; the table is credited to LeSuer 1997); Beia 2024 Table 1, ref. 16 = the 1989 book. Lead to confirm the label counts as naming the primary.
- wathen_1rm (LIF-FIT-014): Lin 2021 Table 1 and worked example, ref. 51; Beia 2024 Table 1, ref. 17.

## CONSUMER DEFECTS
- dapre_endurance: offers an "endurance" DAPRE variant; Knight 1979/1985 (PMID 507268, 4079735) and Greig 2020 (PMC7575491) describe no endurance variant. Severity low (entry is cite-only; Run refuses).
- riegel_race_prediction: uses exponent 1.06 as "Riegel 1981"; Blödorn 2025 (PMC12181339) says the 1981 exponent is 1.07732 and 1.06 is an online calculator's constant; Vickers 2016 (PMC5000509) gives 1.08 elite / 1.05–1.06 recreational men 40–70. Severity low (unconfirmed until the 1981 paper is read).
- iota_simple_rules: entry advertises "Malignancy Risk %" from the 2016 model but carries no coefficients (cite-only). Severity low.

A reliable index for the prognostic significance of blood pressure variability. J Hypertens. 2005;23(3):505-511. doi:10.1097/01.hjh.0000160205.81652.5a. PMID 15716690. Entry computes Σ|xₖ₊₁ − xₖ|/(n − 1) (unweighted); Mazzola 2026 Eur J Transl Myol PMC13389616 prints that form citing Mena; Rouch 2024 J Hypertens PMC11449671 prints a time-weighted form (1/Σw)·Σ wₖ|BPₖ₊₁ − BPₖ|. The PDF decides which is Mena's. BUILD IN num/series | — | none until the weighting is confirmed

## CONSUMER DEFECTS (entry, what it computes, what the paper says, locator, severity)
- bp_child_* / bp_infant / bp_newborn / bp_adolescent (18): one sex-averaged band table ("50th–90th percentile values averaged across sexes"); Flynn 2017 classifies by sex, age and height percentile (Tables 3–5). medium.
- pulse_* / rr_* paediatric bands, pulse_classifier.go, rr_classifier.go: Cleveland Clinic / Iowa bands with Low/Normal/High; Fleming 2011 centiles differ (abstract medians: HR 127 at birth, 145 at ~1 month; RR 44 at birth, 26 at 2 y). medium.
- spo2_oxygenation_index_category: OI edges 5/15/25/40; PALICC 2015 uses 4/8/16 (PMC5253180). medium.
- spo2_adult/child/infant/older/neonatal: unsourced hypoxaemia categories (BTS 2017 gives targets only). medium.
- pulse_qtc_female/male: edges 440/460/470/500; AHA/ACCF/HRS 2009 uses ≥ 450 men / ≥ 460 women (per wave 1; PDF pending). medium.
- bs_random_adult/child: "Normal" 70–140, "Elevated" 141–199, "Critically High" ≥ 300 not in ADA (only ≥ 200 with symptoms). medium. bs_fasting_adult: "Critically High" ≥ 300 unsourced. low.
- temperature_oral_* (6): "Normal"/fever age bands from a patient page; hypothermia core-temperature table applied to oral readings. medium.
- infant_growth_* (8): single oz/in-per-month ranges for both sexes; WHO velocity standards are sex-specific percentiles (MED-PEDS-008 for weight 0–12 months). medium.
- infant_diaper_* / infant_feeding_* (16): edges read from parent-guidance prose. low.
- pulse_chronotropic_response_index_beta_blocker: < 0.62 vs Khan 2005 "≤ 62%". low.
- blood_pressure:all_readings.go: CV from an already-rounded SD (precision loss). low. ComputeTrend / all_readings.go / measurement_weight_trend.go: 2 % stable band, 50 mg/dL swing, CV > 15 %, 5 lb swing unsourced. low.
- bp_adult_*, bp_classifier.go: "Low (Hypotension)" < 90/60, crisis > 180/120, older-adult diastolic floor 55, paediatric 90/50, 120/80 unsourced. low.
- remaining band-only rows (CPP, PVR 3/5 WU, PPI, RPP, shock index, ABI 0.4, adult pulse/RR, athlete, HOMA/QUICKI bands, S/F 235, COPD SpO2, fever metabolic cost): bands with no primary. low.

## doc.go lines for med/respiratory (lead places them)
```
//	MED-RESP-050  PEFAmplitudePercentMean               one day's PEF amplitude percent mean (highest − lowest) / mean × 100, GINA
//	MED-RESP-051  PEFDiurnalVariabilityPercent          average daily diurnal PEF variability over the days given, GINA
//	MED-RESP-052  PEFDiurnalVariabilityExcessiveGINA    whether diurnal PEF variability exceeds GINA Box 1-2 (> 10 % adults, > 13 % children 6–11)
//	MED-RESP-053  PEFChangeConsistentWithAsthmaGINA     whether a PEF change is at least 20 % (GINA, adults)
//	MED-RESP-054  AcuteAsthmaPEFBandSIGN                SIGN 158 Table 15 PEF cell (< 33 / 33–50 / > 50–75 % best or predicted), adults
```

## Assumptions (for doc/ASSUMPTIONS.md via the lead)
- MED-RESP-052: GINA Box 1-2 "Children" read as the box title's "children 6–11 years"; ages ≥ 12 (adolescents) take the "Adults" > 10 %. Alternative: children = < 18 (the consumer's split); lost because the box title groups adolescents with adults.
- MED-RESP-053 and -054 enforce ageYears ≥ 18 ("adults" in the GINA sentence and in SIGN Table 15's title).

## doc.go lines for the lead (existing packages; not edited by this builder)

num/series/doc.go, function list (summary: add "the average real variability, the average rate of change per day,
and the mean of the points in a window of local clock hours"):

	NUM-SERIES-009  AverageRealVariability     mean absolute change between consecutive readings (ARV)
	NUM-SERIES-010  AverageRateOfChangePerDay  (last − first)/elapsed days between two points, the secant slope
	NUM-SERIES-011  LocalHourWindowMean        mean and count of the points in a window of local clock hours

num/stats/doc.go, function list (summary: add "outlier fences, the chi-square goodness-of-fit statistic, and the
standard deviation and median of a frequency table"):

	NUM-STATS-032  TukeyFences                                   quartiles, IQ and inner/outer fences Q1 − 1.5·IQ … Q3 + 3·IQ
	NUM-STATS-033  ChiSquareGoodnessOfFit                        Pearson χ² = Σ (O − E)²/E against expected counts
	NUM-STATS-034  ChiSquareGoodnessOfFitUniform                 Pearson χ² against equal expected counts N/k
	NUM-STATS-035  FrequencyWeightedPopulationStandardDeviation  population SD of a frequency table, √(Σf(x − μ)²/N)
	NUM-STATS-036  FrequencyWeightedSampleStandardDeviation      sample SD of a frequency table, √(Σf(x − x̄)²/(n − 1))
	NUM-STATS-037  FrequencyWeightedMedian                       median of a frequency table without expanding it

num/prob/doc.go, function list (summary: add "the chi-square distribution and likelihood ratios"; the Accuracy
paragraph can add: "the chi-square CDF uses the regularized incomplete gamma function, DLMF 8.7.1 series and 8.9.2
continued fraction, with Loader's prefactor"):

	NUM-PROB-063  ChiSquareCDF                        chi-square P(X ≤ x), regularized incomplete gamma P(ν/2, x/2)
	NUM-PROB-064  ChiSquareSurvival                   chi-square P(X > x), the p-value, accurate in the upper tail
	NUM-PROB-065  ChiSquareQuantile                   chi-square quantile (critical value), safeguarded Newton
	NUM-PROB-066  LikelihoodRatioPositive             LR+ = sensitivity/(1 − specificity)
	NUM-PROB-067  LikelihoodRatioNegative             LR− = (1 − sensitivity)/specificity
	NUM-PROB-068  PostTestProbability                 probability after a result with likelihood ratio LR (odds × LR)

algo/window/doc.go, function list (summary: add "co-occurrence counts of labelled events within a time window"):

	ALG-WINDOW-016  CoOccurrenceWithinWindow        per label pair, events of A with an event of B within the window (sort + two pointers)

## CONFIRM

- None (no PORTED-SECONDARY rows).

## QUESTION FOR DEVIN (sources disagree)

- ARV: Mena 2017 JAHA eq. 1 and Kulkarni 2025 Table 1 give the UNWEIGHTED mean of |ΔBP| (built, NUM-SERIES-009);
  Melgarejo 2024 J Hypertens (PMC11449671, Methods) gives a TIME-WEIGHTED form 1/Σw·Σ w·|ΔBP| (w = interval between
  readings), which is likely Mena 2005's original. Recommendation: keep the unweighted form (what every consumer
  computes, and what the index's author used in 2017); add a time-weighted twin only after the 2005 PDF is read.

## NEEDS PDF

- EdwardsZone / EdwardsTRIMP: Edwards S. The Heart Rate Monitor Book. Sacramento: Fleet Feet Press; 1993 (book; no PMID).
  Home life/fitness.
- MAGE: Service FJ, Molnar GD, Rosevear JW, Ackerman E, Gatewood LC, Taylor WF. Diabetes. 1970;19(9):644-655.
  doi:10.2337/diab.19.9.644. PMID 5469118. Home med/endocrine.
- ARV, time-weighted form (only if wanted beside NUM-SERIES-009): Mena L, Pintos S, Queipo NV, Aizpúrua JA, Maestre G,
  Sulbarán T. A reliable index for the prognostic significance of blood pressure variability. J Hypertens.
  2005;23(3):505-511. doi:10.1097/01.hjh.0000160205.81652.5a. PMID 15716690.

## CONSUMER DEFECTS

- C4 correlation.go:172 DetectSymptomCoOccurrence: CoOccurrenceRate = JointCount/min(CountA, CountB) is documented
  "0.0-1.0" but exceeds 1 (headache ×2 each near the one nausea → 2/1 = 2). No source defines the rate. Severity low.
- C6 unit_convert.go:13-21 KgToLbs/LbsToKg use 2.20462 instead of the exact 1/0.453 592 37 = 2.204 622 62…: relative
  error 1.2e-6 (0.0001 lb at 100 kg). NIST SP 811 App. B.8. Severity low.
- C7 variability.go:42 and bp_trend.go:435 cite Rothwell et al., Lancet Neurol 2010 for ARV; ARV is Mena et al.
  (J Hypertens 2005;23:505; JAHA 2017;6:e006895 eq. 1). Number unchanged; citation wrong. Severity low.
- C5 diurnal.go:34 labels evening ≥ morning as "non-dipping"; dipping is a nocturnal (sleep vs awake) ABPM concept, not
  a morning-vs-evening comparison (triage note; no source supports the AM/PM use). Severity medium (a clinical label
  shown on an unsupported comparison).

## Notes for the reviewer

- Tests use the packages' existing helpers: num/prob `within`, `requireErr`, `requireNoAlloc`, `sinkFloat`, `nan`,
  `inf`; num/stats `near`, `randomFloats`, `benchSizes`, `benchSink`; num/series `newYork`, `closeRel`. New test
  helpers: num/prob `chiSquareTable`, `fmtDF`; num/stats `fifthGradeAges/Freqs`, `expand`, `frequencyBenchInput`;
  num/series `seriesSizeName`.
- ChiSquareCDF relies on the package's `stirlingRemainder` (table ≤ 15 for integers only; it is called only for
  a > 15, where the series form is valid for real a) and `deviance`.
- No test was run by this builder.

Shaw 2023 Innov Aging 7(10):igad076 PMC10714906 (cites Warden 2003); Pinto 2015 Einstein 13(1):14-19 PMC4977604 Chart 1 (cites Warden 2003) | no _test.go / JSON example; TestPAINADScore_parity (all 2 → 10) | bands (≥ 7, ≥ 4, ≥ 1) differ from the only published bands found (Malara 2016, overlapping at 7) (CONSUMER DEFECTS, low)

Counts: PORTED 2 (pecarn_cspine, saps3_score) · PORTED-SECONDARY 5 (revised_trauma_score, nhfs, thakar_score, flacc_scale, painad) · NEEDS PDF 2 (nihss, pawss; plus the nhfs 2012 equation).

## CONFIRM
- revised_trauma_score (MED-TRAUMA-020): Yousefi 2024 PMC11382384 (Table 1 + equation; ref list names Champion 1989); Yu 2021 PMC7959230 (Table 1; ref 13 names Champion 1989). Coefficients also Heiranizadeh 2026 PMC13045056 (names Champion 1989).
- nhfs (MED-TRAUMA-021/022): Lisk 2020 PMC7497295 (ref 9 = Maxwell 2008); Karres 2023 PMC10293368 Table 2 (names Maxwell 2008).
- thakar_score (MED-RENAL-027): Crosina 2017 PMC5678656 Table 1 (names Thakar 2005); Kozioł 2026 PMC12812272 Table 1 (names Thakar 2005).
- flacc_scale (MED-SCALE-014): Uitti 2018 PMC6302518 Table 1 credit line (Merkel 1997); Curtis 2007 PMC1950500 table credit line (Merkel 1997).
- painad (MED-SCALE-015): Shaw 2023 PMC10714906 (ref Warden 2003); Pinto 2015 PMC4977604 (ref Warden 2003).

## NEEDS PDF
- nihss: Brott T, Adams HP Jr, Olinger CP, et al. Measurements of acute cerebral infarction: a clinical examination scale. Stroke. 1989;20(7):864-870. doi:10.1161/01.str.20.7.864. PMID 2749846 — and the NINDS NIH Stroke Scale form (NIH_Stroke_Scale_508C.pdf) for the UN rule.
- pawss: Maldonado JR, Sher Y, Das S, et al. Alcohol Alcohol. 2015;50(5):509-518. doi:10.1093/alcalc/agv043. PMID 25999438 (and Maldonado JR, et al. Alcohol. 2014;48(4):375-390. doi:10.1016/j.alcohol.2014.01.004. PMID 24657098).
- nhfs 2012 mortality equation: Moppett IK, Parker M, Griffiths R, Bowers T, White SM, Moran CG. Br J Anaesth. 2012;109(4):546-550. doi:10.1093/bja/aes187. PMID 22728204.
- (optional, to confirm SAPS 3 combination rule) Moreno 2005 ESM Appendix C, Intensive Care Med 2005;31(10):1345-1355, PMID 16132892.

## CONSUMER DEFECTS
- saps3_score: picker points disagree with Moreno 2005 Table 1 in most rows (AIDS 14, metastatic cancer 17, ER 8, other ICU 5, ward 6, age ≥ 70 lumped at 13, GCS 6–8 13, HR "Not available" 11, …); single-choice comorbidity/reason where the paper adds all that apply. PMC1315315 Tables 1–2. high.
- pecarn_cspine: applies_when 18–120 for a rule enrolled in children < 18 (Leonard 2024 Methods); output labels give imaging advice. PMC11261431. medium.
- nhfs: mortality constant 5.012 taken from a garbled abstract (Karres 2023 prints 5.0122); mortality bands ≥ 20/10/5 % unsourced. PMID 22728204. low.
- revised_trauma_score: cites the 1981 Trauma Score for the 1989 RTS; T-RTS bands (≤ 3, ≤ 10, 11, 12 with triage labels) unsourced. PMID 2657085. low.
- flacc_scale: bands ≥ 7 / ≥ 4 / ≥ 1 sourced only to Univ. Michigan instructions. low.
- painad: bands ≥ 7 / ≥ 4 / ≥ 1 differ from Malara 2016 (overlapping at 7). low.
